# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 012 AFRICA ACTION RUNTIME
# ============================================================================
# Overview:
# - Loads all 102 matrix concepts into one stable profile contract.
# - Quotes and revalidates concrete, target-scaled resource costs.
# - Starts one generation-safe action record and one of four shared missions.
# - Copies the 102-row duration range/default and objective contract into each
#   quote and target record while retaining the shared mission routing bands.
# - Resolves full, partial, failure, and cancellation outcomes idempotently.
# - Keeps AI and player execution on the same begin/resolve/cleanup effects.
# - Routes eight model-gated strange formations through exact templates,
#   manifest receipts, cap/cooldown guards, and explicit refusal flags.
# ============================================================================

		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_duration_maximum = constant:africa_action_contract.ratify_confederal_emergency_action_maximum_days }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_duration_days = constant:africa_action_contract.ratify_confederal_emergency_action_default_days }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_objective = constant:africa_action_contract.ratify_confederal_emergency_action_objective }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.review_covenant_obligation } }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_duration_minimum = constant:africa_action_contract.review_covenant_obligation_minimum_days }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_duration_maximum = constant:africa_action_contract.review_covenant_obligation_maximum_days }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_duration_days = constant:africa_action_contract.review_covenant_obligation_default_days }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_objective = constant:africa_action_contract.review_covenant_obligation_objective }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.hold_postwar_constitutional_review } }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_duration_minimum = constant:africa_action_contract.hold_postwar_constitutional_review_minimum_days }

		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_objective = constant:africa_action_contract.promote_priority_member_package_objective }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_profile_kernel = constant:africa_action_kernel.offer_response } }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_profile_response_days = africa_profile_duration_days }
	}
}

africa_load_action_base_costs = {
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_pp = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }

	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_infantry = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_support = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_motorized = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_trains = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_convoys = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }

	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_civilian = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_intelligence = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_stability = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_war_support = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_profile_cost_tier = constant:africa_value.one } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_pp > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_pp = constant:africa_action_cost.pp_small } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_cp > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_cp = constant:africa_action_cost.cp_small } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_motorized > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_motorized = constant:africa_action_cost.motorized_small } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_trains > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_trains = constant:africa_action_cost.trains_small } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_convoys > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_convoys = constant:africa_action_cost.convoys_small } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_civilian > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_civilian = constant:africa_action_cost.civilian_small } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_intelligence > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_intelligence = constant:africa_action_cost.intelligence_small } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_stability > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_stability = constant:africa_action_cost.stability_small } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_war_support > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_war_support = constant:africa_action_cost.war_support_small } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_profile_cost_tier = constant:africa_value.two } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_pp > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_pp = constant:africa_action_cost.pp_medium } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_cp > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_cp = constant:africa_action_cost.cp_medium } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_manpower > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_manpower = constant:africa_action_cost.manpower_medium } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_infantry > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_infantry = constant:africa_action_cost.infantry_medium } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_support > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_support = constant:africa_action_cost.support_medium } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_motorized > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_motorized = constant:africa_action_cost.motorized_medium } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_trains > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_trains = constant:africa_action_cost.trains_medium } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_convoys > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_convoys = constant:africa_action_cost.convoys_medium } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_fuel > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_fuel = constant:africa_action_cost.fuel_medium } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_cp > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_cp = constant:africa_action_cost.cp_large } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_manpower > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_manpower = constant:africa_action_cost.manpower_large } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_infantry > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_infantry = constant:africa_action_cost.infantry_large } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_support > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_support = constant:africa_action_cost.support_large } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_motorized > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_motorized = constant:africa_action_cost.motorized_large } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_trains > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_trains = constant:africa_action_cost.trains_large } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_convoys > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_convoys = constant:africa_action_cost.convoys_large } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_civilian > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_civilian = constant:africa_action_cost.civilian_large } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_intelligence > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_intelligence = constant:africa_action_cost.intelligence_large } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_stability > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_stability = constant:africa_action_cost.stability_large } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_war_support > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_war_support = constant:africa_action_cost.war_support_large } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_profile_cost_tier = constant:africa_value.four } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_pp > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_pp = constant:africa_action_cost.pp_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_cp > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_cp = constant:africa_action_cost.cp_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_manpower > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_manpower = constant:africa_action_cost.manpower_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_infantry > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_infantry = constant:africa_action_cost.infantry_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_support > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_support = constant:africa_action_cost.support_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_motorized > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_motorized = constant:africa_action_cost.motorized_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_trains > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_trains = constant:africa_action_cost.trains_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_convoys > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_convoys = constant:africa_action_cost.convoys_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_fuel > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_fuel = constant:africa_action_cost.fuel_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_civilian > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_civilian = constant:africa_action_cost.civilian_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_intelligence > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_intelligence = constant:africa_action_cost.intelligence_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_stability > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_stability = constant:africa_action_cost.stability_epic } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_uses_war_support > constant:africa_value.zero } } set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_war_support = constant:africa_action_cost.war_support_epic } }
	}
}

africa_clear_action_quote = {
	clr_country_flag = africa_action_quote_ready
	clear_variable = africa_quote_target_id
	set_variable = { africa_quote_action_id = constant:africa_action.none }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_family = constant:africa_action_family.none }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_kernel = constant:africa_action_kernel.none }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_phase = constant:africa_action_phase.any }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.none }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_band = constant:africa_action_duration_band.instant }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_minimum = constant:africa_action_duration.instant_days }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_maximum = constant:africa_action_duration.instant_days }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_days = constant:africa_action_duration.instant_days }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_response_days = constant:africa_action_duration.instant_days }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_objective = constant:africa_action_objective.none }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_risk = constant:africa_action_risk.medium }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_required_constitution = constant:africa_constitution.uncommitted }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_pp = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_cp = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_manpower = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_infantry = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_support = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_motorized = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_trains = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_convoys = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_fuel = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_civilian = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_intelligence = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_stability = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_war_support = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_natural_disaster_caller_pp = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
	set_variable = { africa_quote_natural_disaster_caller_cp = constant:africa_action_cost.zero }
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host. Input: regular africa_action_target and prepared
# profile. Output: temporary multiplier and concrete component costs.
africa_compute_action_quote = {
	africa_load_action_base_costs = yes
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = constant:africa_value.one }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_target_factory_scale = constant:africa_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_target_state_scale = constant:africa_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_target_confidence_scale = constant:africa_value.zero }

	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = africa_action_target }
		event_target:africa_action_target = {
			set_temp_variable = { africa_target_factory_scale = num_of_factories }
			set_temp_variable = { africa_target_state_scale = num_of_controlled_states }
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = africa_member_confidence }
				set_temp_variable = { africa_target_confidence_scale = africa_member_confidence }
			}
		}
	}

	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = africa_target_factory_scale }
	divide_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = constant:africa_action_cost.target_factory_divisor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = africa_cost_component }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = africa_target_state_scale }
	divide_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = constant:africa_action_cost.target_state_divisor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = africa_cost_component }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_profile_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.state }
				check_variable = { africa_profile_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.region }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = africa_selected_action_state_targets^num }
		divide_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = constant:africa_action_cost.selected_state_divisor }
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = africa_cost_component }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = africa_integration_burden }
	divide_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = constant:africa_action_cost.burden_divisor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = africa_cost_component }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = africa_colonial_pressure }
	divide_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = constant:africa_action_cost.pressure_divisor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = africa_cost_component }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = africa_active_action_count }
	divide_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = constant:africa_action_cost.active_action_divisor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = africa_cost_component }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = africa_target_confidence_scale }
	divide_temp_variable = { africa_cost_component = constant:africa_action_cost.confidence_divisor }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = africa_cost_component }

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_matches_current_host_overlay = yes }
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = constant:africa_action_cost.same_overlay_discount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_profile_required_constitution > constant:africa_constitution.uncommitted }
			check_variable = {
				var = africa_constitution
				value = africa_profile_required_constitution
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = constant:africa_action_cost.route_discount }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = {
				OR = {
					is_neighbor_of = ROOT
					has_military_access_to = ROOT
					ROOT = { has_military_access_to = PREV }
				}
			}
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = constant:africa_action_cost.access_discount }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			NOT = { check_variable = { africa_profile_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.host } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { africa_profile_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.global } }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = constant:africa_action_cost.no_access_surcharge }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_war = yes
				event_target:africa_action_target = { has_war = yes }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_cost_multiplier = constant:africa_action_cost.war_risk_surcharge }
	}
	africa_apply_constitutional_action_cost_adjustment = yes
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = africa_cost_multiplier
		min = constant:africa_action_cost.minimum_multiplier
		max = constant:africa_action_cost.maximum_multiplier
	}

	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_pp = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_cp = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_manpower = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_infantry = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_support = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_motorized = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_trains = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_convoys = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_fuel = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_civilian = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_intelligence = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_stability = africa_cost_multiplier }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_cost_war_support = africa_cost_multiplier }

	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_pp
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_cp
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_manpower
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_infantry
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_support
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_motorized
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_trains
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_convoys
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_fuel
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_civilian
	round_temp_variable = africa_cost_intelligence
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host. Uses the current one-country cursor for human
# actions; host/global actions quote against the host itself.
africa_refresh_selected_action_quote = {
	africa_clear_action_quote = yes
	set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = africa_selected_action_id }
	africa_prepare_action_profile = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_profile_valid > constant:africa_value.zero }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_profile_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.host }
				check_variable = { africa_profile_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.global }
			}
		}
		save_event_target_as = africa_action_target
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_profile_valid > constant:africa_value.zero }
			check_variable = { africa_selected_country_targets^num > constant:africa_value.zero }
		}
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = africa_selected_country_targets
			save_event_target_as = africa_action_target
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_profile_valid > constant:africa_value.zero }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
		}
		africa_compute_action_quote = yes
		set_variable = { africa_quote_action_id = africa_selected_action_id }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_target_id = event_target:africa_action_target.id }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_family = africa_profile_family }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_kernel = africa_profile_kernel }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_phase = africa_profile_phase }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_target_mode = africa_profile_target_mode }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_band = africa_profile_duration_band }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_minimum = africa_profile_duration_minimum }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_maximum = africa_profile_duration_maximum }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_days = africa_profile_duration_days }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_response_days = africa_profile_response_days }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_objective = africa_profile_objective }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_risk = africa_profile_risk }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_required_constitution = africa_profile_required_constitution }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_pp = africa_cost_pp }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_cp = africa_cost_cp }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_manpower = africa_cost_manpower }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_infantry = africa_cost_infantry }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_support = africa_cost_support }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_motorized = africa_cost_motorized }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_trains = africa_cost_trains }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_convoys = africa_cost_convoys }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_fuel = africa_cost_fuel }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_civilian = africa_cost_civilian }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_intelligence = africa_cost_intelligence }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_stability = africa_cost_stability }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_war_support = africa_cost_war_support }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = constant:africa_action.petition_the_rain }
					check_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = constant:africa_action.defy_the_drought }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { africa_quote_natural_disaster_caller_pp = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_pp_cost }
			set_variable = { africa_quote_natural_disaster_caller_cp = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_cp_cost }
		}
		add_to_variable = { africa_action_quote_generation = constant:africa_value.one }
		set_country_flag = africa_action_quote_ready
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on a priority member. This is an explicit refresh requested by
# the member's decision surface. Rain and drought share one bounded enemy
# roster and one target proof flag; registration reads only maintained Event 012
# relationship, Scramble, package, sponsorship, and war-partner arrays.
africa_clear_priority_member_natural_disaster_targets = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = africa_natural_disaster_enemy_targets
		clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_member_target_validated
	}
	clear_array = africa_natural_disaster_enemy_targets
}

africa_register_current_natural_disaster_roster_target = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			africa_natural_disaster_member_target_is_valid = yes
			ROOT = {
				OR = {
					is_in_array = { array = africa_natural_disaster_enemy_targets value = PREV }
					check_variable = {
						var = africa_natural_disaster_enemy_targets^num
						value = constant:africa_capacity.maximum_selected_target_cap
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_member_target_validated
		ROOT = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = africa_natural_disaster_enemy_targets value = PREV } } }
				add_to_array = { array = africa_natural_disaster_enemy_targets value = PREV }
			}
		}
	}
}

africa_refresh_priority_member_natural_disaster_targets = {
	if = {
		limit = { africa_natural_disaster_member_actor_is_eligible = yes }
		africa_clear_priority_member_natural_disaster_targets = yes
		event_target:africa_host = {
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = africa_relationship_countries
				africa_register_current_natural_disaster_roster_target = yes
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = africa_world_package_candidates
				africa_register_current_natural_disaster_roster_target = yes
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = africa_world_package_actors
				africa_register_current_natural_disaster_roster_target = yes
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = africa_scramble_participants
				africa_register_current_natural_disaster_roster_target = yes
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = africa_world_sponsorship_targets
				africa_register_current_natural_disaster_roster_target = yes
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = africa_world_war_registered_partners
				africa_register_current_natural_disaster_roster_target = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on a priority member. The member selects the enemy and pays
# the caller-side reserve; the current Event 012 host then owns the existing
# generation-safe action record and mission lifecycle.
africa_begin_priority_member_natural_disaster_action = {
	set_temp_variable = { africa_priority_member_nature_begin_result = constant:africa_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			africa_natural_disaster_member_actor_is_eligible = yes
			africa_natural_disaster_member_cost_is_available = yes
			has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_member_action_target
			event_target:africa_natural_disaster_member_action_target = {
				africa_natural_disaster_member_target_is_valid = yes
			}
			has_event_target = africa_host
			event_target:africa_host = {
				check_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = constant:africa_action.none }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_action_quote_ready }
			}
		}
		save_global_event_target_as = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor
		event_target:africa_natural_disaster_member_action_target = {
			save_global_event_target_as = africa_action_target
			set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_member_target_validated
		}
		set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_member_nature_action_active
		event_target:africa_host = {
			set_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = ROOT.africa_natural_disaster_member_action_id }
			africa_begin_quoted_action_against_target = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_event_target = africa_action_target
				event_target:africa_action_target = { has_country_flag = africa_action_record_active }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { africa_priority_member_nature_begin_result = constant:africa_value.one }
		}
		else = {
			event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_reserved }
					set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_refund_pp = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_pp_cost }
					add_political_power = africa_natural_disaster_refund_pp
					set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_refund_cp = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_cp_cost }
					add_command_power = africa_natural_disaster_refund_cp
					set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_refund_pp = 0 }
					set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_refund_cp = 0 }
					clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_reserved
				}
				clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_member_nature_action_active
			}
			event_target:africa_host = {
				set_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = constant:africa_action.none }
				africa_clear_action_quote = yes
				africa_clear_selected_action_state_context = yes
			}
			event_target:africa_action_target = { clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_member_target_validated }
			clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_member_nature_action_active
			clear_global_event_target = africa_action_target
			clear_global_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor
		}
		africa_clear_priority_member_natural_disaster_targets = yes
		clear_variable = africa_natural_disaster_member_action_id
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on a priority member. The AI uses the same explicitly
# registered target roster and shared action entry point as the player; it
# never creates a second action ledger or scans the world on a timer/on_action.
africa_priority_member_run_natural_disaster_ai_action = {
	if = {
		limit = { africa_natural_disaster_member_actor_is_eligible = yes }
		africa_refresh_priority_member_natural_disaster_targets = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = africa_natural_disaster_enemy_targets^num
					value = constant:africa_value.zero
					compare = greater_than
				}
			}
			random_scope_in_array = {
				array = africa_natural_disaster_enemy_targets
				save_event_target_as = africa_natural_disaster_member_action_target
			}
			random_list = {
				1 = { set_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_member_action_id = constant:africa_action.petition_the_rain } }
				1 = { set_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_member_action_id = constant:africa_action.defy_the_drought } }
			}
			africa_begin_priority_member_natural_disaster_action = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host. Reserve the caller-side Event 013 payment before
# the shared action ledger is created, so the quoted action cannot start and
# later discover that its hostile nature call is unaffordable. A member caller
# pays from its own stockpile through the same reservation contract.
africa_reserve_natural_disaster_weapon_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_cost_reserved = constant:africa_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.petition_the_rain }
				check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.defy_the_drought }
			}
			has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor
			event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
				africa_natural_disaster_member_cost_is_available = yes
			}
		}
		event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
			set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_payment_pp = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_pp_cost }
			multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_payment_pp = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
			add_political_power = africa_natural_disaster_payment_pp
			set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_payment_cp = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_cp_cost }
			multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_payment_cp = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
			add_command_power = africa_natural_disaster_payment_cp
			set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_reserved
			set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_member_nature_action_active
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_cost_reserved = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.petition_the_rain }
				check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.defy_the_drought }
			}
			NOT = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor }
			africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cost_is_available = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_payment_pp = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_pp_cost }
		multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_payment_pp = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
		add_political_power = africa_natural_disaster_payment_pp
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_payment_cp = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_cp_cost }
		multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_payment_cp = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
		add_command_power = africa_natural_disaster_payment_cp
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_cost_reserved = constant:africa_value.one }
		set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_reserved
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host.
africa_pay_current_action_quote = {
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_pp = africa_quote_cost_pp }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_pp = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_political_power = africa_payment_pp
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_cp = africa_quote_cost_cp }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_cp = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_command_power = africa_payment_cp
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_manpower = africa_quote_cost_manpower }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_manpower = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_manpower = africa_payment_manpower
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_fuel = africa_quote_cost_fuel }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_fuel = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_fuel = africa_payment_fuel
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_stability = africa_quote_cost_stability }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_stability = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_stability = africa_payment_stability
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_war_support = africa_quote_cost_war_support }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_war_support = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_war_support = africa_payment_war_support

	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = africa_quote_cost_infantry }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = africa_payment_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = africa_quote_cost_support }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = africa_payment_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = africa_quote_cost_motorized }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = africa_payment_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = africa_quote_cost_trains }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = africa_payment_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = africa_quote_cost_convoys }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_payment_equipment = constant:africa_action_cost.negation_multiplier }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = africa_payment_equipment }

	subtract_from_variable = { africa_civilian_capacity_available = africa_quote_cost_civilian }
	subtract_from_variable = { africa_intelligence_capacity_available = africa_quote_cost_intelligence }
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host. Input: temporary africa_requested_action_family.
africa_select_action_family_page = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_family > constant:africa_action_family.none }
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_family < constant:africa_value.fifteen }
		}
		set_variable = { africa_selected_action_family = africa_requested_action_family }
		set_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = constant:africa_action.none }
		africa_clear_action_quote = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host. Input: temporary africa_requested_action_id.
africa_select_action_for_quote = {
	africa_prepare_action_profile = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_profile_valid > constant:africa_value.zero }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_first_proof_active }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_first_proof_failed }
				}
				africa_first_proof_requested_action_is_allowed = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = africa_requested_action_id }
		set_variable = { africa_selected_action_family = africa_profile_family }
		africa_refresh_selected_action_state_candidates = yes
		africa_refresh_selected_action_quote = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host. Compatibility wrapper retained for older callers;
# the executable profile selects the exact matrix action without scanning for
# a substitute target or reducing the playbook to a coarse proof family.
africa_select_mapped_first_proof_action = {
	africa_select_playbook_first_proof_action = yes
}
# Input: prepared profile and regular africa_action_target. Output is one only
# when the exact matrix concept can start against the current target.
africa_validate_action_specific_requirements = {
	set_temp_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result = constant:africa_value.zero }
	# 1. guarantee_sovereignty
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.guarantee_sovereignty }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_action_target_is_usable = yes }
			event_target:africa_action_target = { OR = { africa_relationship_is_outside = yes africa_relationship_is_protected = yes } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	# 2. open_aid_corridor
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.open_aid_corridor }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_action_target_is_usable = yes }
			event_target:africa_action_target = { OR = { africa_relationship_is_protected = yes africa_relationship_is_associate = yes africa_relationship_is_chartered = yes africa_relationship_is_autonomous_federal = yes has_country_flag = africa_first_proof_aid_target } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	# 3. dispatch_charter_mission
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.dispatch_charter_mission }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_action_target_is_usable = yes }
			event_target:africa_action_target = { OR = { africa_relationship_is_outside = yes africa_relationship_is_protected = yes africa_relationship_is_associate = yes } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	# 4. deploy_volunteers
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.deploy_volunteers }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_action_target_is_usable = yes }
			event_target:africa_action_target = { has_war = yes }
			event_target:africa_action_target = { OR = { africa_relationship_is_outside = yes africa_relationship_is_protected = yes africa_relationship_is_associate = yes africa_relationship_is_chartered = yes } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	# 5. intervene_against_coloniser
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.intervene_against_coloniser }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_action_target_is_usable = yes }
			event_target:africa_action_target = {
				OR = {
					AND = { africa_is_valid_external_action_target = yes has_war = yes }
					AND = { has_country_flag = africa_first_proof_punitive_partner has_war = yes }
					has_country_flag = africa_first_proof_punitive_coloniser
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	# 6. evacuate_leaders_archives
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.evacuate_leaders_archives }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_action_target_is_usable = yes }
			event_target:africa_action_target = { surrender_progress > constant:africa_action_threshold.near_capitulation }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	# 7. recognise_provisional_government
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.recognise_provisional_government }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_action_target_is_usable = yes }
			event_target:africa_action_target = { OR = { is_subject = yes has_country_flag = africa_contact_liberation_priority has_country_flag = africa_liberation_movement_candidate } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	# 8. secure_border_sanctuary
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.secure_border_sanctuary }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_action_target_is_usable = yes }
			event_target:africa_action_target = { is_neighbor_of = ROOT }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	# 9. break_blockade
	if = {
		limit = {

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Action start and shared mission activation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY scope on the host. Rebuilds the quote against the exact launch target
# so a target or world-state change can never spend a stale displayed cost.
africa_requote_selected_action_against_action_target = {
	africa_clear_action_quote = yes
	set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = africa_selected_action_id }
	africa_prepare_action_profile = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { africa_profile_valid > constant:africa_value.zero }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
		}
		africa_compute_action_quote = yes
		set_variable = { africa_quote_action_id = africa_selected_action_id }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_target_id = event_target:africa_action_target.id }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_family = africa_profile_family }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_kernel = africa_profile_kernel }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_phase = africa_profile_phase }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_target_mode = africa_profile_target_mode }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_band = africa_profile_duration_band }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_minimum = africa_profile_duration_minimum }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_maximum = africa_profile_duration_maximum }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_duration_days = africa_profile_duration_days }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_response_days = africa_profile_response_days }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_objective = africa_profile_objective }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_risk = africa_profile_risk }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_required_constitution = africa_profile_required_constitution }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_pp = africa_cost_pp }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_cp = africa_cost_cp }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_manpower = africa_cost_manpower }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_infantry = africa_cost_infantry }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_support = africa_cost_support }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_motorized = africa_cost_motorized }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_trains = africa_cost_trains }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_convoys = africa_cost_convoys }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_fuel = africa_cost_fuel }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_civilian = africa_cost_civilian }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_intelligence = africa_cost_intelligence }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_stability = africa_cost_stability }
		set_variable = { africa_quote_cost_war_support = africa_cost_war_support }
		add_to_variable = { africa_action_quote_generation = constant:africa_value.one }
		set_country_flag = africa_action_quote_ready
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host. State projects consume one explicit map cursor;
# region projects consume one to four deliberate states. There is no random
# geography substitute, and every selected state must remain valid at launch.
africa_prepare_action_state_target = {
	set_temp_variable = { africa_action_state_target_valid = constant:africa_value.one }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_profile_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.state } }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_state_target_valid = constant:africa_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { africa_selected_action_state_targets^num = constant:africa_value.one }
				all_of_scopes = {
					array = africa_selected_action_state_targets
					has_state_flag = africa_selected_action_state
					has_state_flag = africa_selected_action_state_candidate
					NOT = { has_state_flag = africa_action_project_active }
					OR = {
						is_on_continent = africa
						ROOT = { check_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = constant:africa_action.administer_world_regions } }
					}
					is_controlled_by = event_target:africa_action_target
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { africa_action_state_target_valid = constant:africa_value.one }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_profile_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.region } }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_state_target_valid = constant:africa_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { africa_selected_action_state_targets^num > constant:africa_value.zero }
				NOT = { check_variable = { africa_selected_action_state_targets^num > constant:africa_action_capacity.maximum_region_state_targets } }
				all_of_scopes = {
					array = africa_selected_action_state_targets
					has_state_flag = africa_selected_action_state
					has_state_flag = africa_selected_action_state_candidate
					NOT = { has_state_flag = africa_action_project_active }
					OR = {
						is_on_continent = africa
						ROOT = { check_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = constant:africa_action.administer_world_regions } }
					}
					is_controlled_by = event_target:africa_action_target
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { africa_action_state_target_valid = constant:africa_value.one }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host. Human choices are written by the Charter League
# interface. AI hosts select a real bounded programme parameter before the
# quote is validated, preferring an origin or skill not yet represented.
africa_prepare_diaspora_action_parameters = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.charter_passage_programme }
		}
		set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_origin_group = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.none }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.africa_achievement_diaspora_origin_groups value = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.north_america } }
			}
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_origin_group = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.north_america }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.africa_achievement_diaspora_origin_groups value = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.south_america_caribbean } }
			}
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_origin_group = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.south_america_caribbean }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.africa_achievement_diaspora_origin_groups value = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.europe } }
			}
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_origin_group = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.europe }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.africa_achievement_diaspora_origin_groups value = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.indian_ocean } }
			}
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_origin_group = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.indian_ocean }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_origin_group = constant:africa_achievement_origin_group.north_america }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.invite_afro_american_technical_mission }
		}
		set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_skill_programme = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.none }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.africa_achievement_diaspora_skill_programmes value = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.education } }
			}
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_skill_programme = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.education }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.africa_achievement_diaspora_skill_programmes value = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.medicine } }
			}
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_skill_programme = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.medicine }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.africa_achievement_diaspora_skill_programmes value = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.engineering } }
			}
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_skill_programme = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.engineering }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.africa_achievement_diaspora_skill_programmes value = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.administration } }
			}
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_skill_programme = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.administration }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { africa_selected_diaspora_skill_programme = constant:africa_achievement_skill_programme.education }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host after payment. Copies the immutable action snapshot
# to the target and creates reverse links.
africa_create_action_record = {
	set_temp_variable = { africa_effective_action_risk = africa_quote_risk }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = africa_charter_authority
				value = constant:africa_measure.high
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			event_target:africa_action_target = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = africa_member_confidence }
					check_variable = {
						var = africa_member_confidence
						value = constant:africa_measure.high
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
			}
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { africa_effective_action_risk = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = {
					var = africa_integration_burden
					value = constant:africa_measure.critical
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = africa_colonial_pressure
					value = constant:africa_measure.critical
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_effective_action_risk = constant:africa_value.one }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = africa_effective_action_risk
		min = constant:africa_action_risk.low
		max = constant:africa_action_risk.extreme
	}
	add_to_variable = { africa_action_generation = constant:africa_value.one }
	add_to_variable = { africa_active_action_count = constant:africa_value.one }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_action_targets value = event_target:africa_action_target } } }
		add_to_array = { array = africa_active_action_targets value = event_target:africa_action_target }
	}
	event_target:africa_action_target = {
		set_country_flag = africa_action_record_active
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { ROOT.africa_quote_action_id = constant:africa_action.petition_the_rain }
					check_variable = { ROOT.africa_quote_action_id = constant:africa_action.defy_the_drought }
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_call_accepted_recorded
			clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_call_rejected_recorded
		}
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_id = ROOT.africa_quote_action_id }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_family = ROOT.africa_quote_family }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_kernel = ROOT.africa_quote_kernel }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_status = constant:africa_action_status.active }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.none }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_generation = ROOT.africa_action_generation }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_host_generation = ROOT.africa_host_generation }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_duration_band = ROOT.africa_quote_duration_band }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_duration_minimum = ROOT.africa_quote_duration_minimum }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_duration_maximum = ROOT.africa_quote_duration_maximum }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_duration_days = ROOT.africa_quote_duration_days }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_response_days = ROOT.africa_quote_response_days }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_objective = ROOT.africa_quote_objective }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_started_at_war = constant:africa_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { has_war = yes }
			set_variable = { africa_active_action_started_at_war = constant:africa_value.one }
		}
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_target_mode = ROOT.africa_quote_target_mode }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_risk = africa_effective_action_risk }
		africa_apply_constitutional_action_risk_adjustment = yes
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_pp = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_pp }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_cp = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_cp }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_manpower = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_manpower }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_infantry = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_infantry }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_support = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_support }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_motorized = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_motorized }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_trains = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_trains }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_convoys = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_convoys }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_fuel = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_fuel }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_civilian_reservation = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_civilian }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_intelligence_reservation = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_intelligence }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_stability = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_stability }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_cost_war_support = ROOT.africa_quote_cost_war_support }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_natural_disaster_cost_reserved = ROOT.africa_natural_disaster_cost_reserved }
		set_variable = { africa_active_diaspora_origin_group = ROOT.africa_selected_diaspora_origin_group }
		set_variable = { africa_active_diaspora_skill_programme = ROOT.africa_selected_diaspora_skill_programme }
		clear_array = africa_action_state_targets
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_quote_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.state }
				check_variable = { africa_quote_target_mode = constant:africa_action_target_mode.region }
			}
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = africa_selected_action_state_targets
			set_state_flag = africa_action_project_active
			set_variable = { africa_action_project_generation = event_target:africa_action_target.africa_active_action_generation }
			set_variable = { africa_action_project_host_generation = event_target:africa_action_target.africa_active_action_host_generation }
			event_target:africa_action_target = { add_to_array = { array = africa_action_state_targets value = PREV } }
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_action_states value = PREV } } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = africa_active_action_states value = PREV } }
			}
		}
	}
	event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_diaspora_open_withdrawal_review = yes }
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host.
africa_activate_shared_action_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_quote_duration_band = constant:africa_action_duration_band.short } }
		add_to_array = { array = africa_active_action_short_targets value = event_target:africa_action_target }
		event_target:africa_action_target = { set_country_flag = africa_action_duration_short }
		activate_targeted_decision = { target = event_target:africa_action_target decision = mission_africa_action_short }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_quote_duration_band = constant:africa_action_duration_band.medium } }
		add_to_array = { array = africa_active_action_medium_targets value = event_target:africa_action_target }
		event_target:africa_action_target = { set_country_flag = africa_action_duration_medium }
		activate_targeted_decision = { target = event_target:africa_action_target decision = mission_africa_action_medium }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_quote_duration_band = constant:africa_action_duration_band.long } }
		add_to_array = { array = africa_active_action_long_targets value = event_target:africa_action_target }
		event_target:africa_action_target = { set_country_flag = africa_action_duration_long }
		activate_targeted_decision = { target = event_target:africa_action_target decision = mission_africa_action_long }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_quote_duration_band = constant:africa_action_duration_band.epic } }
		add_to_array = { array = africa_active_action_epic_targets value = event_target:africa_action_target }
		event_target:africa_action_target = { set_country_flag = africa_action_duration_epic }
		activate_targeted_decision = { target = event_target:africa_action_target decision = mission_africa_action_epic }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the host. Input: regular africa_action_target.
africa_begin_quoted_action_against_target = {
	set_temp_variable = { africa_action_begin_result = constant:africa_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_cost_reserved = constant:africa_value.zero }
	africa_requote_selected_action_against_action_target = yes
	set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = africa_selected_action_id }
	africa_prepare_action_profile = yes
	africa_prepare_diaspora_action_parameters = yes
	africa_prepare_action_state_target = yes
	africa_prepare_first_proof_action_markers = yes
	africa_prepare_first_proof_recovery_contract = yes
	africa_validate_action_specific_requirements = yes
	africa_diaspora_validate_target_capacity = yes
	africa_diaspora_prepare_target_response = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			africa_is_current_host = yes
			africa_has_open_action_capacity = yes
			africa_action_quote_phase_is_available = yes
			africa_can_pay_action_quote = yes
			check_variable = { africa_action_state_target_valid > constant:africa_value.zero }
			check_variable = { africa_action_specific_validation_result > constant:africa_value.zero }
			check_variable = { africa_diaspora_capacity_validation_result > constant:africa_value.zero }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_diaspora_request_pending }
			has_event_target = africa_action_target
			event_target:africa_action_target = { NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_action_record_active } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_requested_action_id = constant:africa_action.recover_failed_host_proof } }
			africa_begin_first_proof_recovery_contract = yes
		}
		africa_reserve_natural_disaster_weapon_cost = yes
		africa_pay_current_action_quote = yes
		africa_create_action_record = yes
		event_target:africa_action_target = { africa_achievement_record_action_start = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_quote_duration_band = constant:africa_action_duration_band.instant } }
			event_target:africa_action_target = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.form_dynamic_two_continent_union } }
					africa_roll_action_outcome = yes
				}
				else = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.full } }
				africa_resolve_action = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			africa_activate_shared_action_mission = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_action_begin_result = constant:africa_value.one }
		set_variable = { africa_selected_action_id = constant:africa_action.none }
		africa_clear_action_quote = yes
		africa_clear_selected_action_state_context = yes
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_diaspora_request_pending }
			}
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr12.221 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on an active target. Shared mission timeout dispatches the
# response event in the target scope, avoiding delayed-event backlogs when a
# country temporarily ceases to exist.
africa_timeout_current_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			africa_action_response_is_current = yes
		}
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr12.210 }
	}
	else = {
		africa_resolve_action = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the resolved target. Records every one of the 102 matrix
# dispositions without creating per-action runtime stores.
africa_record_action_partial_disposition = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.guarantee_sovereignty } }
		set_country_flag = africa_action_guarantee_sovereignty_partial
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_action_guarantee_sovereignty_partial_recorded }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.open_aid_corridor } }
		set_country_flag = africa_action_open_aid_corridor_partial
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_action_open_aid_corridor_partial_recorded }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.dispatch_charter_mission } }
		set_country_flag = africa_action_dispatch_charter_mission_partial
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_action_dispatch_charter_mission_partial_recorded }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.deploy_volunteers } }
		set_country_flag = africa_action_deploy_volunteers_partial
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_action_deploy_volunteers_partial_recorded }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.intervene_against_coloniser } }
		set_country_flag = africa_action_intervene_against_coloniser_partial
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_action_intervene_against_coloniser_partial_recorded }
