# COUNTRY scope on the resolved target.
africa_apply_full_action_family_values = {
	if = {
		limit = { africa_is_valid_relationship_target = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = africa_member_confidence } } set_variable = { africa_member_confidence = constant:africa_measure.medium } }
		add_to_variable = { africa_member_confidence = constant:africa_action_result.full_confidence }
		clamp_variable = { var = africa_member_confidence min = constant:africa_measure.minimum max = constant:africa_measure.maximum }
	}
	event_target:africa_host = {
		add_to_variable = { africa_charter_authority = constant:africa_action_result.full_authority }
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.protection } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_continental_reach = constant:africa_action_result.full_reach }
			add_to_variable = { africa_colonial_pressure = constant:africa_action_result.full_pressure_provocation }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.accession } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_consent_commitment = constant:africa_action_result.full_consent }
			add_to_variable = { africa_integration_burden = constant:africa_action_result.full_burden }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.regional_congress } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_regional_equality = constant:africa_action_result.full_equality }
			add_to_variable = { africa_member_representation_balance = constant:africa_action_result.full_capacity }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.integration } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_central_capacity = constant:africa_action_result.full_capacity }
			add_to_variable = { africa_integration_burden = constant:africa_action_result.full_burden }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.economy } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_continental_reach = constant:africa_action_result.full_reach }
			add_to_variable = { africa_civilian_capacity_available = constant:africa_value.one }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.diaspora } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_diaspora_trust = constant:africa_action_result.full_trust }
			add_to_variable = { africa_diaspora_settlement_capacity = constant:africa_action_result.full_capacity }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.rival_bloc } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_colonial_pressure = constant:africa_action_result.full_pressure_reduction }
			add_to_variable = { africa_consent_commitment = constant:africa_action_result.full_consent }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.high_chaos } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_ecological_wrath = constant:africa_action_result.full_wrath_reduction }
			add_to_variable = { africa_continental_war_readiness = constant:africa_action_result.full_readiness }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.scramble } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_colonial_pressure = constant:africa_action_result.full_pressure_reduction }
			add_to_variable = { africa_continental_war_readiness = constant:africa_action_result.full_readiness }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.world_order } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_continental_reach = constant:africa_action_result.full_reach }
			add_to_variable = { africa_integration_burden = constant:africa_action_result.full_burden }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				PREV = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.constitutional_crisis }
						check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.post_unification }
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { africa_emergency_rule_debt = constant:africa_action_result.full_debt_reduction }
			add_to_variable = { africa_consent_commitment = constant:africa_action_result.full_consent }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.host_opening } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_continental_reach = constant:africa_action_result.full_reach }
			add_to_variable = { africa_consent_commitment = constant:africa_action_result.full_consent }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.regional_restorations } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_restoration_legitimacy = constant:africa_action_result.full_legitimacy }
			add_to_variable = { africa_regional_equality = constant:africa_action_result.full_equality }
		}
		africa_clamp_host_values = yes
	}
}

africa_apply_partial_action_family_values = {
	if = {
		limit = { africa_is_valid_relationship_target = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = africa_member_confidence } } set_variable = { africa_member_confidence = constant:africa_measure.medium } }
		add_to_variable = { africa_member_confidence = constant:africa_action_result.partial_confidence }
		clamp_variable = { var = africa_member_confidence min = constant:africa_measure.minimum max = constant:africa_measure.maximum }
	}
	event_target:africa_host = {
		add_to_variable = { africa_integration_burden = constant:africa_action_result.partial_burden }
		add_to_variable = { africa_colonial_pressure = constant:africa_action_result.partial_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { africa_emergency_rule_debt = constant:africa_action_result.partial_debt }
		africa_clamp_host_values = yes
	}
}

africa_apply_failed_action_family_values = {
	if = {
		limit = { africa_is_valid_relationship_target = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = africa_member_confidence } } set_variable = { africa_member_confidence = constant:africa_measure.medium } }
		add_to_variable = { africa_member_confidence = constant:africa_action_result.failure_confidence }
		clamp_variable = { var = africa_member_confidence min = constant:africa_measure.minimum max = constant:africa_measure.maximum }
	}
	event_target:africa_host = {
		add_to_variable = { africa_charter_authority = constant:africa_action_result.failure_authority }
		add_to_variable = { africa_integration_burden = constant:africa_action_result.failure_burden }
		add_to_variable = { africa_colonial_pressure = constant:africa_action_result.failure_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { africa_emergency_rule_debt = constant:africa_action_result.failure_debt }
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.high_chaos } } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_ecological_wrath = constant:africa_action_result.failure_wrath }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = africa_rival_pressure } } set_variable = { africa_rival_pressure = constant:africa_measure.minimum } }
		add_to_variable = { africa_rival_pressure = constant:africa_action_result.failure_rival_pressure }
		clamp_variable = { var = africa_rival_pressure min = constant:africa_measure.minimum max = constant:africa_measure.maximum }
		africa_clamp_host_values = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = africa_ecological_wrath
					value = constant:africa_achievement_ratio.ecological_rampage_threshold
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			africa_achievement_record_weather_wrath_collapse = yes
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Matrix-specific full-result payloads
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY scope on a valid African target. These helpers traverse only accepted
# graph edges and never infer consent from opinion.
africa_action_transition_to_protected = {
	if = {
		limit = { africa_relationship_is_outside = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.protected }
		africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
	}
}

africa_action_transition_to_associate = {
	if = {
		limit = { africa_relationship_is_outside = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.associate }
		africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				africa_relationship_is_protected = yes
				africa_relationship_is_leaving = yes
				africa_relationship_is_rival_bloc = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.associate }
		africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
	}
}

africa_action_transition_to_chartered = {
	africa_action_transition_to_associate = yes
	if = {
		limit = { africa_relationship_is_associate = yes }
		set_country_flag = africa_member_accession_clause_accepted
		set_country_flag = africa_member_charter_admission_accepted
		set_country_flag = africa_member_continental_constitution_accepted
		set_country_flag = africa_member_representation_settled
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:africa_host = { check_variable = { africa_constitution = constant:africa_constitution.continental_confederation } } }
			set_country_flag = africa_member_confederal_unity_recognised
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.chartered }
		africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { africa_relationship_is_resistant = yes }
		set_country_flag = africa_member_accession_clause_accepted
		set_country_flag = africa_member_charter_admission_accepted
		set_country_flag = africa_member_continental_constitution_accepted
		set_country_flag = africa_member_representation_settled
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:africa_host = { check_variable = { africa_constitution = constant:africa_constitution.continental_confederation } } }
			set_country_flag = africa_member_confederal_unity_recognised
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.chartered }
		africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
	}
}

africa_action_transition_to_autonomous_federal = {
	africa_action_transition_to_chartered = yes
	set_country_flag = africa_member_integration_consent
	set_country_flag = africa_member_representation_settled
	set_country_flag = africa_member_administrative_dossier_complete
	set_country_flag = africa_member_autonomy_statute_accepted
	set_country_flag = africa_member_continental_constitution_accepted
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = africa_member_confidence } }
		set_variable = { africa_member_confidence = constant:africa_measure.medium }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_member_confidence < constant:africa_measure.medium } }
		set_variable = { africa_member_confidence = constant:africa_measure.medium }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				africa_relationship_is_chartered = yes
				africa_relationship_is_integrated_region = yes
				africa_relationship_is_rival_bloc = yes
				africa_relationship_is_occupied_settlement = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.autonomous_federal }
		africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on the resolved target.
# STATE scope inside an active action target's immutable state array. A rail
# proof exists only when the engine can build and then observe a real
# connection from that country's capital to the selected state.
africa_build_selected_rail_spine_connection = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_capital = yes }
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			ROOT = {
				can_build_railway = {
					level = constant:africa_action_building.rail_level
					start_state = ROOT.capital
					target_state = PREV
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = {
			build_railway = {
				level = constant:africa_action_building.rail_level
				start_state = ROOT.capital
				target_state = PREV
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_railway_connection = {
						start_state = ROOT.capital
						target_state = PREV
					}
				}
			}
			set_state_flag = africa_rail_spine_connection_operational
			ROOT = { set_country_flag = africa_regional_rail_spine_operational }
		}
	}
}

# STATE scope. River and lake routes are grounded in the curated map registry
# above; the infrastructure represents quays, ferries, feeder roads, and cargo
# handling attached to that actual waterway.
africa_build_selected_river_or_lake_transport = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			africa_state_is_registered_river_or_lake_project_site = yes
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = infrastructure
			level = constant:africa_action_building.infrastructure_minor
			instant_build = yes
		}
		set_state_flag = africa_river_or_lake_transport_operational
		ROOT = { set_country_flag = africa_river_transport_operational }
	}
}

# STATE scope. A port modernisation can only operate on an existing naval-base
# site, preventing an inland selection from receiving a paper result.
africa_build_selected_port_modernisation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			is_coastal = yes
			naval_base > constant:africa_value.zero
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = naval_base
			level = constant:africa_action_building.port_minor
			instant_build = yes
		}
		set_state_flag = africa_port_modernisation_operational
		ROOT = { set_country_flag = africa_continental_port_modernised }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. The caller selects one Event 012 strange-force family in
# africa_strange_force_requested_kind. This guard owns the global package gate,
# per-family model/entity/counter/audio manifest contract, the eight-formation
# cap, and the exact template/entity consumer. It never sets the package-ready
# flag and never substitutes a vanilla or generic formation when a manifest is
# absent.
africa_strange_force_spawn_guard = {
	save_event_target_as = africa_strange_force_spawn_owner
	set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_spawn_result = constant:africa_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_spawn_refusal = constant:africa_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.africa_strange_force_formation_count } }
		set_variable = { global.africa_strange_force_formation_count = constant:africa_value.zero }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_equipment_factor = constant:africa_strange_force.formation_equipment_factor }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_manpower_factor = constant:africa_strange_force.formation_manpower_factor }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_experience_factor = constant:africa_strange_force.formation_experience_factor }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = africa_strange_formation_package_ready
			check_variable = { var = global.africa_strange_force_formation_count value = constant:africa_strange_force.formation_cap compare = less_than }
			any_owned_state = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = event_target:africa_strange_force_spawn_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.stone_cohorts }
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_stone_cohorts_model_manifest_ready
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_stone_cohorts_entity_manifest_ready
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_stone_cohorts_counter_manifest_ready
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_stone_cohorts_audio_manifest_ready
				NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_strange_force_stone_cohorts_spawned_once }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_strange_force_stone_cohorts_cooldown }
			}
			set_technology = { africa_stone_cohorts_tech = 1 popup = no }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = africa_stone_cohorts_equipment_1
				amount = constant:africa_strange_force.stone_cohorts_stockpile
			}
			division_template = {
				name = "Africa Strange Stone Cohorts"
				is_locked = yes
				force_allow_recruiting = no
				override_model = chaosx_stone_cohorts
				regiments = {
					stone_cohorts = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					stone_cohorts = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				}
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = event_target:africa_strange_force_spawn_owner }
				meta_effect = {
					text = {
						create_unit = {
							division = "name = \"Africa Strange Stone Cohorts\" division_template = \"Africa Strange Stone Cohorts\" start_experience_factor = [STRANGE_FORCE_EXPERIENCE] start_equipment_factor = [STRANGE_FORCE_EQUIPMENT] start_manpower_factor = [STRANGE_FORCE_MANPOWER]"
						}
						owner = event_target:africa_strange_force_spawn_owner
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					}
					STRANGE_FORCE_EXPERIENCE = "[?africa_strange_force_experience_factor|.2]"
					STRANGE_FORCE_EQUIPMENT = "[?africa_strange_force_equipment_factor|.2]"
					STRANGE_FORCE_MANPOWER = "[?africa_strange_force_manpower_factor|.2]"
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_spawn_result = constant:africa_value.one }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.gorilla_heavy_infantry }
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_gorilla_heavy_infantry_model_manifest_ready
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_gorilla_heavy_infantry_entity_manifest_ready
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_gorilla_heavy_infantry_counter_manifest_ready
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_gorilla_heavy_infantry_audio_manifest_ready
				NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_strange_force_gorilla_heavy_infantry_spawned_once }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_strange_force_gorilla_heavy_infantry_cooldown }
			}
			set_technology = { africa_gorilla_heavy_infantry_tech = 1 popup = no }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = africa_gorilla_heavy_infantry_equipment_1

			}

				override_model = disaster_wardens
				regiments = {
					infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				}
				support = {
					disaster_wardens = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				}
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = event_target:africa_strange_force_spawn_owner }
				meta_effect = {
					text = {
						create_unit = {
							division = "name = \"Africa Strange Disaster Wardens\" division_template = \"Africa Strange Disaster Wardens\" start_experience_factor = [STRANGE_FORCE_EXPERIENCE] start_equipment_factor = [STRANGE_FORCE_EQUIPMENT] start_manpower_factor = [STRANGE_FORCE_MANPOWER]"
						}
						owner = event_target:africa_strange_force_spawn_owner
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					}
					STRANGE_FORCE_EXPERIENCE = "[?africa_strange_force_experience_factor|.2]"
					STRANGE_FORCE_EQUIPMENT = "[?africa_strange_force_equipment_factor|.2]"
					STRANGE_FORCE_MANPOWER = "[?africa_strange_force_manpower_factor|.2]"
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_spawn_result = constant:africa_value.one }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.plague_carriers }
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_plague_carriers_model_manifest_ready
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_plague_carriers_entity_manifest_ready
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_plague_carriers_counter_manifest_ready
				has_global_flag = africa_strange_force_plague_carriers_audio_manifest_ready
				NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_strange_force_plague_carriers_spawned_once }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_strange_force_plague_carriers_cooldown }
			}
			set_technology = { africa_plague_carriers_tech = 1 popup = no }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = africa_plague_carriers_equipment_1
				amount = constant:africa_strange_force.plague_carriers_stockpile
			}
			division_template = {
				name = "Africa Strange Plague Carriers"
				is_locked = yes
				force_allow_recruiting = no
				override_model = plague_carriers
				regiments = {
					plague_carriers = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					plague_carriers = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				}
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = event_target:africa_strange_force_spawn_owner }
				meta_effect = {
					text = {
						create_unit = {
							division = "name = \"Africa Strange Plague Carriers\" division_template = \"Africa Strange Plague Carriers\" start_experience_factor = [STRANGE_FORCE_EXPERIENCE] start_equipment_factor = [STRANGE_FORCE_EQUIPMENT] start_manpower_factor = [STRANGE_FORCE_MANPOWER]"
						}
						owner = event_target:africa_strange_force_spawn_owner
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					}
					STRANGE_FORCE_EXPERIENCE = "[?africa_strange_force_experience_factor|.2]"
					STRANGE_FORCE_EQUIPMENT = "[?africa_strange_force_equipment_factor|.2]"
					STRANGE_FORCE_MANPOWER = "[?africa_strange_force_manpower_factor|.2]"
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_spawn_result = constant:africa_value.one }
		}
	}
	africa_strange_force_finalize_result = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope. Finalises one guarded formation attempt. The permanent
# receipt prevents duplicate templates for the same family; the short timed
# receipt also protects repeated action/focus callbacks during the same chain.
africa_strange_force_finalize_result = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_spawn_result > constant:africa_value.zero } }
		add_to_variable = { global.africa_strange_force_formation_count = constant:africa_value.one }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_cooldown_days = constant:africa_action_threshold.cooldown_days }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.stone_cohorts } }
			set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_stone_cohorts_spawned_once
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_strange_force_stone_cohorts_cooldown days = var:africa_strange_force_cooldown_days }
			scoped_sound_effect = "chaosx_stone_cohorts_select_sfx"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.gorilla_heavy_infantry } }
			set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_gorilla_heavy_infantry_spawned_once
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_strange_force_gorilla_heavy_infantry_cooldown days = var:africa_strange_force_cooldown_days }
			scoped_sound_effect = "chaosx_gorilla_heavy_infantry_select_sfx"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.pan_sappers } }
			set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_pan_sappers_spawned_once
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_strange_force_pan_sappers_cooldown days = var:africa_strange_force_cooldown_days }
			scoped_sound_effect = "chaosx_pan_sappers_select_sfx"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.riverborn } }
			set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_riverborn_spawned_once
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_strange_force_riverborn_cooldown days = var:africa_strange_force_cooldown_days }
			scoped_sound_effect = "chaosx_riverborn_select_sfx"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.forest_giants } }
			set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_forest_giants_spawned_once
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_strange_force_forest_giants_cooldown days = var:africa_strange_force_cooldown_days }
			scoped_sound_effect = "chaosx_forest_giants_select_sfx"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.oracle_recon } }
			set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_oracle_recon_spawned_once
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_strange_force_oracle_recon_cooldown days = var:africa_strange_force_cooldown_days }
			scoped_sound_effect = "chaosx_oracle_recon_select_sfx"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.disaster_wardens } }
			set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_disaster_wardens_spawned_once
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_strange_force_disaster_wardens_cooldown days = var:africa_strange_force_cooldown_days }
			scoped_sound_effect = "chaosx_disaster_wardens_select_sfx"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.plague_carriers } }
			set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_plague_carriers_spawned_once
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_strange_force_plague_carriers_cooldown days = var:africa_strange_force_cooldown_days }
			scoped_sound_effect = "chaosx_plague_carriers_select_sfx"
		}
		clear_variable = africa_strange_force_cooldown_days
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_spawn_refusal = constant:africa_value.one }
		set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_runtime_refused
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.stone_cohorts } } set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_stone_cohorts_runtime_refused }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.gorilla_heavy_infantry } } set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_gorilla_heavy_infantry_runtime_refused }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.pan_sappers } } set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_pan_sappers_runtime_refused }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.riverborn } } set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_riverborn_runtime_refused }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.forest_giants } } set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_forest_giants_runtime_refused }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.oracle_recon } } set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_oracle_recon_runtime_refused }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.disaster_wardens } } set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_disaster_wardens_runtime_refused }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.plague_carriers } } set_country_flag = africa_strange_force_plague_carriers_runtime_refused }
	}
}

# Narrow callers keep route/action semantics readable and make each exact
# consumer auditable without allowing a dynamic unit or entity fallback.

	set_temp_variable = { africa_strange_force_requested_kind = constant:africa_strange_force_kind.stone_cohorts }
	africa_strange_force_spawn_guard = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope on the active target after the shared Event 013 bridge. The
# natural-disaster API remains authoritative: Wardens are attempted only after
# an accepted call and are owned by the exact registered actor when present.
africa_strange_force_spawn_disaster_wardens_after_natural_disaster = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.petition_the_rain }
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.defy_the_drought }
			}
			has_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_call_accepted_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor }
			event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = { africa_strange_force_spawn_disaster_wardens = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = africa_host }
			event_target:africa_host = { africa_strange_force_spawn_disaster_wardens = yes }
		}
	}
}

africa_apply_action_full_semantics = {
	# Protection: concrete guarantees, material corridors, rescue records, and
	# bounded state or naval access projects.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.guarantee_sovereignty } }
		set_country_flag = africa_protection_guarantee_active
		set_country_flag = africa_member_requested_protection
		africa_action_transition_to_protected = yes
		event_target:africa_host = { give_guarantee = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.open_aid_corridor } }
		set_country_flag = africa_aid_corridor_operational
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:africa_action_material_transfer.aid_infantry }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:africa_action_material_transfer.aid_support }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.dispatch_charter_mission } }
		set_country_flag = africa_accession_preferences_revealed
		set_country_flag = africa_charter_mission_received
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = africa_first_proof_diplomatic_recognition_target }
			set_country_flag = africa_diplomatic_recognition_proof_completed
		}
		event_target:africa_host = {
			if = { limit = { africa_has_dossier_capacity = yes NOT = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_dossiers value = PREV } } } add_to_array = { array = africa_active_dossiers value = PREV } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.deploy_volunteers } }
		set_country_flag = africa_charter_volunteer_contingent_present
		add_manpower = constant:africa_action_material_transfer.volunteer_manpower
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:africa_action_material_transfer.volunteer_equipment }
		set_country_flag = africa_lifetime_received_volunteers
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.intervene_against_coloniser } }
		set_country_flag = africa_targeted_intervention_authorized
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_targeted_colonial_war_plan_ready }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.evacuate_leaders_archives } }
		set_country_flag = africa_government_and_archives_preserved
		set_country_flag = africa_restoration_claim_preserved
		event_target:africa_host = { add_to_variable = { africa_restoration_legitimacy = constant:africa_action_result.full_legitimacy } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.recognise_provisional_government } }
		set_country_flag = africa_liberation_movement_recognised
		set_country_flag = africa_provisional_government_has_league_access
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.secure_border_sanctuary } }
		set_country_flag = africa_border_sanctuary_supported
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = africa_action_state_targets set_state_flag = africa_liberation_sanctuary_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.break_blockade } }
		set_country_flag = africa_blockade_corridor_open
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = constant:africa_action_material_transfer.aid_support }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.emergency_relief_column } }
		clr_country_flag = africa_relief_crisis_active
		set_country_flag = africa_relief_column_arrived
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = constant:africa_action_material_transfer.relief_motorized }
	}

	# Accession: an accepted offer moves through the relationship graph and writes
	# the exact negotiated clauses. Opinion never enters these branches.
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.offer_defence_charter } }
		set_country_flag = africa_clause_common_defence
		africa_action_transition_to_associate = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.offer_development_charter } }
		set_country_flag = africa_clause_development_priority
		africa_action_transition_to_associate = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.offer_federal_charter } }
		set_country_flag = africa_clause_federal_autonomy
		africa_action_transition_to_autonomous_federal = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.offer_crown_charter } }
		set_country_flag = africa_clause_preserved_crown
		set_country_flag = africa_member_succession_rules_settled
		africa_action_transition_to_chartered = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.offer_peoples_charter } }
		set_country_flag = africa_clause_mass_congress_representation
		africa_action_transition_to_chartered = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.offer_security_charter } }
		set_country_flag = africa_clause_common_command
		set_country_flag = africa_clause_command_sunset
		africa_action_transition_to_chartered = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.offer_sacred_ecological_compact } }
		set_country_flag = africa_clause_ecological_obligation
		set_country_flag = africa_high_chaos_compact_active
		africa_action_transition_to_associate = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.renegotiate_accession_clauses } }
		set_country_flag = africa_accession_clauses_renegotiated
		set_country_flag = africa_member_continental_constitution_accepted
		if = {
			limit = {
				africa_relationship_is_chartered = yes
				event_target:africa_host = { check_variable = { africa_constitution = constant:africa_constitution.continental_confederation } }
			}
			set_country_flag = africa_member_confederal_unity_recognised
		}
		clr_country_flag = africa_departure_pressure_active
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.hold_accession_referendum } }
		set_country_flag = africa_accession_referendum_recognised
		set_country_flag = africa_member_integration_consent
		africa_action_transition_to_associate = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.admit_member_in_emergency } }
		set_country_flag = africa_emergency_admission_active
		set_country_flag = africa_postwar_constitutional_review_required
		africa_action_transition_to_associate = yes
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_postwar_constitutional_review_pending }
	}

	# Congress and restoration outcomes remain negotiated, retain opt-outs, and
	# commit only the selected region or polity.
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.convene_regional_congress } }
		set_country_flag = africa_active_congress_delegate
		event_target:africa_host = {
			if = { limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_congress_regions value = PREV } } } add_to_array = { array = africa_active_congress_regions value = PREV } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.settle_overlapping_claims } }
		set_country_flag = africa_overlap_settlement_accepted
		clr_country_flag = africa_overlap_dispute_active
		africa_register_selected_continental_peace_exemption = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.create_regional_charter } }
		set_country_flag = africa_regional_charter_established
		africa_action_transition_to_chartered = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.form_regional_federation } }
		set_country_flag = africa_regional_federation_established
		africa_action_transition_to_autonomous_federal = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.restore_historical_polity } }
		set_country_flag = africa_restored_polity_member
		set_country_flag = africa_restoration_legitimacy_recognised
		set_country_flag = africa_member_allied_settlement_accepted
		set_country_flag = africa_member_continental_constitution_accepted
		africa_action_transition_to_associate = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.approve_direct_integration_schedule } }
		set_country_flag = africa_direct_integration_schedule_approved
		set_country_flag = africa_member_representation_settled
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.guarantee_regional_representation } }
		set_country_flag = africa_member_representation_settled
		set_country_flag = africa_regional_seat_guarantee
		if = {
			limit = {
				africa_is_current_host = yes
				check_variable = { africa_host_playbook = constant:africa_host_playbook.comoros }
				has_country_flag = africa_first_proof_active
			}
			set_country_flag = africa_first_proof_comoros_grande_comore_seat
			set_country_flag = africa_first_proof_comoros_anjouan_seat
			set_country_flag = africa_first_proof_comoros_moheli_seat
			set_country_flag = africa_first_proof_comoros_mayotte_seat
			custom_effect_tooltip = africa_comoros_equal_island_representation_tt
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.fund_congress_security } }
		set_country_flag = africa_congress_security_consent_obtained
		clr_country_flag = africa_congress_sabotage_risk
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.invite_diaspora_delegates } }
		set_country_flag = africa_diaspora_delegates_represented
