# COUNTRY scope on the resolved target.
africa_apply_action_partial_semantics = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.protection } }
		set_country_flag = africa_limited_protection_result
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.guarantee_sovereignty } }
			set_country_flag = africa_limited_guarantee_active
			set_country_flag = africa_member_requested_protection
			africa_action_transition_to_protected = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.open_aid_corridor } }
			set_country_flag = africa_aid_corridor_attrition
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:africa_action_material_transfer.aid_support }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.evacuate_leaders_archives } }
			set_country_flag = africa_partial_evacuation_preserved_archives
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.accession } }
		set_country_flag = africa_accession_counteroffer_pending
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.offer_defence_charter } }
			set_country_flag = africa_observer_and_guarantee_only
			africa_action_transition_to_protected = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.offer_development_charter } }
			set_country_flag = africa_bilateral_project_without_membership
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.admit_member_in_emergency } }
			set_country_flag = africa_emergency_protection_only
			africa_action_transition_to_protected = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.regional_congress }
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.regional_restorations }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = africa_congress_partial_settlement
		set_country_flag = africa_congress_opt_out_preserved
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.promote_priority_member_package } }
			set_country_flag = africa_priority_partial_autonomy
			set_country_flag = africa_clause_federal_autonomy
			clr_country_flag = africa_priority_promotion_candidate
			if = {
				limit = { africa_relationship_is_outside = yes }
				africa_action_transition_to_protected = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { africa_relationship_is_protected = yes }
				africa_action_transition_to_associate = yes
			}
			africa_priority_member_record_partial_requalification_issue = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.integration } }
		set_country_flag = africa_integration_project_incomplete
		set_country_flag = africa_autonomy_extended_pending_review
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.economy } }
		set_country_flag = africa_economic_project_partial_capacity
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = africa_action_state_targets
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = constant:africa_action_building.infrastructure_minor instant_build = yes }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.diaspora } }
		set_country_flag = africa_diaspora_observer_or_limited_programme
		set_country_flag = africa_return_requires_individual_consent
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.rival_bloc } }
		set_country_flag = africa_rival_temporary_truce
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.high_chaos } }
		set_country_flag = africa_high_chaos_temporary_boundary
		set_country_flag = africa_high_chaos_followup_required
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.scramble } }
		set_country_flag = africa_scramble_response_delayed_not_resolved
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.world_order } }
		set_country_flag = africa_world_order_alliance_without_union
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.constitutional_crisis }
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.post_unification }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = africa_constitutional_temporary_rule
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_constitutional_followup_crisis_required }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.recover_failed_host_proof } }
		event_target:africa_host = {
			set_country_flag = africa_first_proof_recovery_partial_result
			add_to_variable = { africa_first_proof_recovery_attempts = constant:africa_value.one }
		}
	}
	africa_world_order_apply_partial_result = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope on the resolved target.
africa_apply_action_failure_semantics = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.protection } }
		set_country_flag = africa_protection_action_failed
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.guarantee_sovereignty } }
			set_country_flag = africa_lifetime_broken_guarantee
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.evacuate_leaders_archives } }
			set_country_flag = africa_evacuation_route_lost
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.accession } }
		set_country_flag = africa_accession_offer_refused
		set_country_flag = africa_refusal_behaviour_recorded
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.renegotiate_accession_clauses }
				OR = {
					africa_relationship_is_chartered = yes
					africa_relationship_is_autonomous_federal = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.leaving }
			africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.regional_congress }
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.regional_restorations }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = africa_congress_settlement_failed
		set_country_flag = africa_overlap_dispute_active
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.promote_priority_member_package } }
			set_country_flag = africa_priority_promotion_refused
			set_country_flag = africa_priority_overlap_or_access_failure
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					africa_relationship_is_chartered = yes
					africa_relationship_is_autonomous_federal = yes
					africa_relationship_is_integrated_region = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.resistant }
			africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { africa_relationship_is_resistant = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.rival_bloc }
			africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.integration } }
		set_country_flag = africa_integration_project_resisted
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					africa_relationship_is_chartered = yes
					africa_relationship_is_autonomous_federal = yes
					africa_relationship_is_integrated_region = yes
					africa_relationship_is_occupied_settlement = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.resistant }
			africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.economy } }
		set_country_flag = africa_economic_project_failed
		set_country_flag = africa_project_access_damaged
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.diaspora } }
		set_country_flag = africa_diaspora_programme_refused_or_unsafe
		set_country_flag = africa_forced_relocation_forbidden
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.rival_bloc } }
		set_country_flag = africa_rival_crisis_escalated
		if = {
			limit = { africa_relationship_is_leaving = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { africa_requested_relationship_state = constant:africa_relationship.rival_bloc }
			africa_apply_relationship_transition = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.high_chaos } }
		set_country_flag = africa_high_chaos_actor_hostile
		set_country_flag = africa_containment_breach_active
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.scramble } }
		set_country_flag = africa_scramble_expedition_accelerated
		set_country_flag = africa_scramble_coalition_member
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.world_order } }
		set_country_flag = africa_world_order_rivalry_intensified
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.prepare_continental_war } }
			set_country_flag = africa_continental_rival_first_strike_ready
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.constitutional_crisis }
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_family = constant:africa_action_family.post_unification }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = africa_constitutional_crisis_escalated
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_route_viability_under_review }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.recover_failed_host_proof } }
		event_target:africa_host = {
			add_to_variable = { africa_first_proof_recovery_attempts = constant:africa_value.one }
			set_country_flag = africa_normal_early_mandate_lost
			set_country_flag = africa_regional_coalition_rebuild_required
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.promote_priority_member_package } }
		set_country_flag = africa_priority_promotion_refused
		set_country_flag = africa_priority_overlap_or_access_failure
	}
	africa_world_order_apply_failure_result = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope on the Event 012 host. The caller seeds the action and
# payment reservation from the exact active action target before invoking
# this helper. The selected-country event target is never widened or rerolled.
africa_prepare_hostile_natural_disaster_strength = {
	set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.base_strength }

	# Route flags provide a floor, with the strongest completed route taking
	# precedence instead of stacking every intermediate flag.
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_covenant_route_capstone }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.capstone_route_strength }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_covenant_write_warfare_doctrine }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.warfare_route_strength }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_covenant_favour_and_wrath }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.favour_route_strength }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_covenant_route_committed }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.committed_route_strength }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_priority_member_full_promotion }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.priority_route_strength }
	}

	# The extra nature-call payment and the drought action's longer, extreme
	# profile each add one bounded point after the route floor is selected.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cost_reserved > constant:africa_value.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.caller_cost_strength }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_action_id = constant:africa_action.defy_the_drought } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.drought_action_strength }
	}
	# A priority member's package authority is an explicit actor signal. The
	# host still owns the shared ledger, but the member's bounded nature ladder
	# raises the Event 013 impact envelope so stronger packages hit harder.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor
			event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
				has_variable = africa_priority_member_nature_power
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_member_power = event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor.africa_priority_member_nature_power }
		multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_member_power = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.priority_member_nature_strength_per_level }
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = africa_natural_disaster_member_power }
		set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_member_power = 0 }
	}

	# Pressure and war-readiness are read from the live host ledger. Existing
	# Africa measure bands keep the thresholds shared with the rest of Event 012.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = africa_ecological_wrath
			check_variable = { africa_ecological_wrath > constant:africa_measure.low }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.ecological_wrath_low_strength }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = africa_ecological_wrath
			check_variable = { africa_ecological_wrath > constant:africa_measure.medium }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.ecological_wrath_medium_strength }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = africa_ecological_wrath
			check_variable = { africa_ecological_wrath > constant:africa_measure.high }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.ecological_wrath_high_strength }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = africa_continental_war_readiness
			check_variable = { africa_continental_war_readiness > constant:africa_measure.medium }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.war_readiness_medium_strength }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = africa_continental_war_readiness
			check_variable = { africa_continental_war_readiness > constant:africa_measure.high }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.war_readiness_high_strength }
	}

	# The exact selected enemy's size is an impact-envelope adjustment, not an
	# actor-power signal, and contributes at most one point per axis. This reads
	# only the saved event target and never searches for another country/state.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country
			event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_country = {
				num_of_factories > constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.target_factory_threshold
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.target_size_strength }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country
			event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_country = {
				num_of_controlled_states > constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.target_state_threshold
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.target_size_strength }
	}

	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength
		min = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.base_strength
		max = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.maximum_strength
	}
	set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength_delta = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength_delta = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.base_strength }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength_delta
		min = constant:africa_value.zero
		max = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.maximum_strength
	}

	# Convert strength points to all five public Event 013 impact scales. The
	# Event 013 wrapper validates these values against its own shared bounds.
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_death_scale = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.base_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.base_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_warning_scale = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.base_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.base_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_supply_scale = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.base_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength_delta }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.death_scale_per_strength }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_death_scale = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength_delta }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.building_scale_per_strength }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength_delta }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.warning_scale_per_strength }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_warning_scale = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength_delta }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.recovery_scale_per_strength }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta }
	set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength_delta }
	multiply_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta = constant:africa_natural_disaster_strength.supply_scale_per_strength }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_supply_scale = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_scale_delta }

	clamp_temp_variable = { var = natural_disaster_call_death_scale min = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum max = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = natural_disaster_call_building_scale min = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum max = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = natural_disaster_call_warning_scale min = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum max = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale min = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum max = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = natural_disaster_call_supply_scale min = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.minimum max = constant:natural_disaster_call_scale.maximum }
}

# COUNTRY scope on the resolved target. Uses the existing action target and
# host event targets; Event 013 receives one exact selected-country target and
# its public hostile_actor contract. No new target array or world iteration is
# introduced here.
africa_call_hostile_natural_disaster_from_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.petition_the_rain }
				check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.defy_the_drought }
			}
			has_variable = africa_active_action_natural_disaster_cost_reserved
			check_variable = { africa_active_action_natural_disaster_cost_reserved > constant:africa_value.zero }
			has_event_target = africa_host
			NOT = { africa_is_current_host = yes }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_war_with = event_target:africa_host
					event_target:africa_host = {
						africa_natural_disaster_weapon_actor_is_eligible = yes
						has_war = yes
						has_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_reserved
						NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cooldown }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor
					event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
						africa_natural_disaster_member_actor_is_eligible = yes
						has_war_with = ROOT
						has_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_reserved
						NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cooldown }
					}
					event_target:africa_host = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_actor_is_eligible = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_call_target_country
		event_target:africa_host = {
			set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_action_id = PREV.africa_active_action_id }
			set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cost_reserved = PREV.africa_active_action_natural_disaster_cost_reserved }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.hostile_actor }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_event_id }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.random }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_country }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied = constant:africa_value.one }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.defy_the_drought } } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = africa_priority_member_full_promotion }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = africa_covenant_write_warfare_doctrine }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = africa_covenant_route_capstone }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor
					event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
						check_variable = { africa_priority_member_nature_power > constant:africa_priority_member_nature_power.high }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor
					NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_covenant_route_capstone }
					event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
						check_variable = { africa_priority_member_nature_power > constant:africa_priority_member_nature_power.low }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
			}
			africa_prepare_hostile_natural_disaster_strength = yes
			set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_strength = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_strength }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.single }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count = constant:africa_value.one }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.meaningful }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.affected_country }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.normal }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.family }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_log_mode = constant:natural_disaster_log_mode.event_013_history }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cost_checked = constant:africa_value.one }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cooldown_checked = constant:africa_value.one }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_legitimacy_checked = constant:africa_value.one }
			set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_caller_cost_checked
			set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_caller_cooldown_checked
			set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_target_legitimacy_checked
			call_natural_disaster = yes
			set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_result = natural_disaster_call_result }
			set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = natural_disaster_call_reject_reason }
			set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_sequence_id = natural_disaster_call_sequence_id }
			set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_primary_family = natural_disaster_call_resolved_primary_family }
			set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_primary_job_count = natural_disaster_call_primary_job_count }
			set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_skipped_primary_count = natural_disaster_call_skipped_primary_count }
			set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days = constant:africa_natural_disaster.cooldown_days }
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cooldown days = var:africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days }
			clear_variable = africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor }
				event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
					set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_result = event_target:africa_host.africa_last_natural_disaster_call_result }
					set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = event_target:africa_host.africa_last_natural_disaster_call_reject_reason }
					set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_sequence_id = event_target:africa_host.africa_last_natural_disaster_call_sequence_id }
					set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_primary_family = event_target:africa_host.africa_last_natural_disaster_call_primary_family }
					set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_primary_job_count = event_target:africa_host.africa_last_natural_disaster_call_primary_job_count }
					set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_skipped_primary_count = event_target:africa_host.africa_last_natural_disaster_call_skipped_primary_count }
					set_variable = { africa_last_natural_disaster_call_strength = event_target:africa_host.africa_last_natural_disaster_call_strength }
					set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days = constant:africa_natural_disaster.cooldown_days }
					set_country_flag = { flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cooldown days = var:africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days }
					clear_variable = africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_result = constant:natural_disaster_call_result.accepted } }
				set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_call_accepted
				PREV = { set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_call_accepted_recorded }
				if = { limit = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor } event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = { set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_call_accepted } }
				# Event 013 accepted the exact hostile selected-country call. The
				# target classifications below are disqualifier receipts only; a
				# campaign victory still requires the target to capitulate to this host.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_has_resolved_primary_country > constant:africa_value.zero } }
						event_target:natural_disaster_call_resolved_primary_country = {
							set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_targeted
							set_variable = { var = africa_natural_disaster_target_host_generation value = event_target:africa_host.africa_host_generation }
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							event_target:natural_disaster_call_resolved_primary_country = {
								OR = {
									africa_member_host_generation_is_current = yes
									africa_relationship_is_cooperative = yes
								}
							}
						}
						africa_achievement_record_weather_member_disaster = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							event_target:natural_disaster_call_resolved_primary_country = {
								capital_scope = { is_on_continent = africa }
								africa_relationship_is_outside = yes
							}
						}
						africa_achievement_record_weather_neutral_african = yes
					}
				}
					random = {
					chance = constant:africa_natural_disaster.backfire_chance
					set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_backfire_recorded
					if = { limit = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor } event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = { set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_backfire_recorded } }
					africa_achievement_record_weather_campaign_backfire = yes
					add_to_variable = { africa_ecological_wrath = constant:africa_natural_disaster.backfire_wrath }
					africa_clamp_host_values = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								var = africa_ecological_wrath
								value = constant:africa_achievement_ratio.ecological_rampage_threshold
								compare = greater_than_or_equals
							}
						}
						africa_achievement_record_weather_wrath_collapse = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_call_rejected
				PREV = { set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_call_rejected_recorded }
				if = { limit = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor } event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = { set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_call_rejected } }
				set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_backfire_recorded
				if = { limit = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor } event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = { set_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_backfire_recorded } }
				africa_achievement_record_weather_campaign_backfire = yes
				add_to_variable = { africa_ecological_wrath = constant:africa_natural_disaster.backfire_wrath }
				africa_clamp_host_values = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = africa_ecological_wrath
							value = constant:africa_achievement_ratio.ecological_rampage_threshold
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					africa_achievement_record_weather_wrath_collapse = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Outcome roll, resolution, and idempotent cleanup
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY scope on an active action target.
africa_roll_action_outcome = {
	set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.none }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.declare_the_world_is_one } }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.full }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_risk = constant:africa_action_risk.low } }
		random_list = {
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.low_full = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.full } }
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.low_partial = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.partial } }
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.low_failure = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.failure } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_risk = constant:africa_action_risk.medium } }
		random_list = {
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.medium_full = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.full } }
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.medium_partial = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.partial } }
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.medium_failure = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.failure } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_risk = constant:africa_action_risk.high } }
		random_list = {
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.high_full = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.full } }
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.high_partial = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.partial } }
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.high_failure = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.failure } }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.extreme_full = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.full } }
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.extreme_partial = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.partial } }
			constant:africa_action_outcome_weight.extreme_failure = { set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.failure } }
		}
	}
}

# Legacy proof success used a broad action-ID shortcut. Exact proof resolution
# now runs once from africa_resolve_action through the compound ledger.
# COUNTRY scope on an active action target.
africa_apply_current_action_outcome = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.full } }
		africa_record_action_full_disposition = yes
		africa_apply_full_action_family_values = yes
		africa_apply_action_full_semantics = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.contain_emergent_disease } }
			africa_disease_apply_containment_full = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.research_disease_countermeasure } }
			event_target:africa_host = { africa_disease_apply_research_full = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.weaponise_fictional_pathogen } }
			africa_disease_apply_weapon_full = yes
		}
		africa_call_hostile_natural_disaster_from_action = yes
		africa_strange_force_spawn_disaster_wardens_after_natural_disaster = yes
		africa_achievement_record_full_action = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.partial } }
		africa_record_action_partial_disposition = yes
		africa_apply_partial_action_family_values = yes
		africa_apply_action_partial_semantics = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.contain_emergent_disease } }
			africa_disease_apply_containment_partial = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.research_disease_countermeasure } }
			event_target:africa_host = { africa_disease_apply_research_partial = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.weaponise_fictional_pathogen } }
			africa_disease_apply_weapon_partial = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.failure } }
		africa_record_action_failure_disposition = yes
		africa_apply_failed_action_family_values = yes
		africa_apply_action_failure_semantics = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.contain_emergent_disease } }
			africa_disease_apply_containment_failure = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.research_disease_countermeasure } }
			event_target:africa_host = { africa_disease_apply_research_failure = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_id = constant:africa_action.weaponise_fictional_pathogen } }
			africa_disease_apply_weapon_failure = yes
		}
	}
	africa_diaspora_apply_current_outcome = yes
	africa_achievement_record_action_outcome = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope on an active target. Removes the exact shared mission before
# clearing the duration flag.
africa_remove_current_action_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_action_duration_short }
		event_target:africa_host = { remove_targeted_decision = { target = PREV decision = mission_africa_action_short } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_action_duration_medium }
		event_target:africa_host = { remove_targeted_decision = { target = PREV decision = mission_africa_action_medium } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_action_duration_long }
		event_target:africa_host = { remove_targeted_decision = { target = PREV decision = mission_africa_action_long } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_action_duration_epic }
		event_target:africa_host = { remove_targeted_decision = { target = PREV decision = mission_africa_action_epic } }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on an active target. This effect is safe to call more than once.
africa_cleanup_action = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_action_record_active }
		africa_diaspora_cleanup_action_state = yes
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_id = africa_active_action_id }
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_outcome = africa_active_action_outcome }
		# Preserve the immutable row-specific objective for the result event.
		# The live objective is cleared below with the rest of the active record;
		# retaining this snapshot keeps the action log and detail window aligned
		# with the matrix row that actually ran.
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_objective = africa_active_action_objective }
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_generation = africa_active_action_generation }
		set_country_flag = africa_has_action_history
		africa_remove_current_action_mission = yes
		africa_disease_cleanup_target_receipts = yes
		event_target:africa_host = {
			clear_variable = africa_disease_seed_result
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { PREV.africa_active_action_natural_disaster_cost_reserved > constant:africa_value.zero } }
				clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_reserved
				# A weather attempt has consumed the caller reserve even when the
				# shared action resolves partial or failed and therefore never calls
				# Event 013. Preserve the same actor cooldown used by the full bridge;
				# the guard avoids extending a cooldown already written by that bridge.
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cooldown } }
					set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days = constant:africa_natural_disaster.cooldown_days }
					set_country_flag = { flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cooldown days = var:africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days }
					clear_variable = africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor }
					event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
						if = {
							limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cooldown } }
							set_temp_variable = { africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days = constant:africa_natural_disaster.cooldown_days }
							set_country_flag = { flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_cooldown days = var:africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days }
							clear_variable = africa_natural_disaster_cooldown_days
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor }
				event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
					clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_weapon_reserved
					clr_country_flag = africa_natural_disaster_member_nature_action_active
				}
				clear_global_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor
				clear_global_event_target = africa_action_target
			}
			if = { limit = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_action_targets value = PREV } } remove_from_array = { array = africa_active_action_targets value = PREV } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_action_short_targets value = PREV } } remove_from_array = { array = africa_active_action_short_targets value = PREV } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_action_medium_targets value = PREV } } remove_from_array = { array = africa_active_action_medium_targets value = PREV } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_action_long_targets value = PREV } } remove_from_array = { array = africa_active_action_long_targets value = PREV } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_action_epic_targets value = PREV } } remove_from_array = { array = africa_active_action_epic_targets value = PREV } }
			add_to_variable = { africa_civilian_capacity_available = PREV.africa_active_action_civilian_reservation }
			add_to_variable = { africa_intelligence_capacity_available = PREV.africa_active_action_intelligence_reservation }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_count > constant:africa_value.zero } }
				subtract_from_variable = { africa_active_action_count = constant:africa_value.one }
			}
			clamp_variable = { var = africa_civilian_capacity_available min = constant:africa_value.zero max = constant:africa_action_capacity.maximum_civilian_capacity }
			clamp_variable = { var = africa_intelligence_capacity_available min = constant:africa_value.zero max = constant:africa_action_capacity.maximum_intelligence_capacity }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = africa_action_state_targets
			event_target:africa_host = {
				if = { limit = { is_in_array = { array = africa_active_action_states value = PREV } } remove_from_array = { array = africa_active_action_states value = PREV } }
			}
			clr_state_flag = africa_action_project_active
			clear_variable = africa_action_project_generation
			clear_variable = africa_action_project_host_generation
		}
		clear_array = africa_action_state_targets
		set_variable = { africa_action_cooldown_days = constant:africa_action_threshold.cooldown_days }
		set_country_flag = { flag = africa_action_recent_target days = var:africa_action_cooldown_days }
		clear_variable = africa_action_cooldown_days
		clr_country_flag = africa_action_record_active
		clr_country_flag = africa_action_duration_short
		clr_country_flag = africa_action_duration_medium
		clr_country_flag = africa_action_duration_long
		clr_country_flag = africa_action_duration_epic
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_id
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_family
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_kernel
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_status
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_outcome
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_generation
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_host_generation
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_duration_band
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_duration_minimum
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_duration_maximum
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_duration_days
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_response_days
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_objective
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_started_at_war
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_target_mode
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_risk
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_pp
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_cp
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_manpower
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_infantry
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_support
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_motorized
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_trains
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_convoys
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_fuel
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_civilian_reservation
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_intelligence_reservation
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_stability
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_war_support
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_natural_disaster_cost_reserved
		clear_variable = africa_active_action_state_id
		clear_variable = africa_active_diaspora_origin_group
		clear_variable = africa_active_diaspora_skill_programme
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on an active target.
africa_resolve_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			africa_active_action_generation_is_current = yes
			check_variable = { africa_active_action_status = constant:africa_action_status.active }
		}
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_status = constant:africa_action_status.resolving }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.none } }
			africa_roll_action_outcome = yes
		}
		africa_apply_current_action_outcome = yes
		africa_handle_first_proof_action_resolution = yes
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_status = constant:africa_action_status.resolved }
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_id = africa_active_action_id }
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_outcome = africa_active_action_outcome }
		# Snapshot the row-specific objective before the result popup is created.
		# The popup evaluates its scripted localisation immediately, while the
		# idempotent cleanup below clears the active record afterward.
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_objective = africa_active_action_objective }
		save_event_target_as = africa_action_result_target
		event_target:africa_host = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr12.220 } }
		africa_cleanup_action = yes
		event_target:africa_host = { africa_focus_ai_retry_pending_route_action = yes }
		event_target:africa_host = { africa_focus_ai_retry_overlay_corridor_action = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on an action target that is still present during on_annex.
# Current-generation records use the normal cancel owner. A stale or
# hostless record cannot safely refund a successor host, so it receives only
# target-local teardown; state locks are cleared only when both stored
# generations still match the victim's immutable action receipt.
africa_cleanup_annexed_action_target = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_action_record_active }
		if = {
			limit = { africa_active_action_generation_is_current = yes }
			africa_cancel_action = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { africa_last_action_id = africa_active_action_id }
			set_variable = { africa_last_action_outcome = africa_active_action_outcome }
			set_variable = { africa_last_action_objective = africa_active_action_objective }
			set_variable = { africa_last_action_generation = africa_active_action_generation }
			set_country_flag = africa_has_action_history
			africa_diaspora_cleanup_action_state = yes
			africa_disease_cleanup_target_receipts = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = africa_host }
				africa_unregister_current_relationship_state = yes
				event_target:africa_host = { africa_reconcile_relationship_counts_and_caps = yes }
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = africa_action_state_targets
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = africa_action_project_active
						has_variable = africa_action_project_generation
						has_variable = africa_action_project_host_generation
						ROOT = {
							has_variable = africa_active_action_generation
							has_variable = africa_active_action_host_generation
						}
						check_variable = { var = africa_action_project_generation value = ROOT.africa_active_action_generation compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = africa_action_project_host_generation value = ROOT.africa_active_action_host_generation compare = equals }
					}
					clr_state_flag = africa_action_project_active
					clear_variable = africa_action_project_generation
					clear_variable = africa_action_project_host_generation
				}
			}
			clear_array = africa_action_state_targets
			set_variable = { africa_action_cooldown_days = constant:africa_action_threshold.cooldown_days }
			set_country_flag = { flag = africa_action_recent_target days = var:africa_action_cooldown_days }
			clear_variable = africa_action_cooldown_days
			clr_country_flag = africa_action_record_active
			clr_country_flag = africa_action_duration_short
			clr_country_flag = africa_action_duration_medium
			clr_country_flag = africa_action_duration_long
			clr_country_flag = africa_action_duration_epic
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_id
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_family
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_kernel
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_status
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_outcome
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_generation
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_host_generation
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_duration_band
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_duration_minimum
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_duration_maximum
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_duration_days
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_response_days
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_objective
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_started_at_war
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_target_mode
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_risk
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_pp
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_cp
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_manpower
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_infantry
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_support
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_motorized
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_trains
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_convoys
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_fuel
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_civilian_reservation
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_intelligence_reservation
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_stability
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_cost_war_support
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_natural_disaster_cost_reserved
			clear_variable = africa_active_action_state_id
			clear_variable = africa_active_diaspora_origin_group
			clear_variable = africa_active_diaspora_skill_programme
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = africa_action_target }
				event_target:africa_action_target = {
					if = { limit = { tag = ROOT } clear_global_event_target = africa_action_target }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor }
				event_target:africa_natural_disaster_action_actor = {
					if = { limit = { tag = ROOT } clear_global_event_target = africa_natural_disaster_action_actor }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on an active target.
africa_cancel_action = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = africa_action_record_active }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_outcome = constant:africa_action_outcome.cancelled }
		set_variable = { africa_active_action_status = constant:africa_action_status.cancelled }
		set_country_flag = africa_action_cancelled_with_cleanup
		event_target:africa_host = { set_country_flag = africa_any_action_cancelled_with_cleanup }
		africa_diaspora_apply_cancel_cleanup = yes
		if = {
			limit = { africa_is_valid_relationship_target = yes has_variable = africa_member_confidence }
			add_to_variable = { africa_member_confidence = constant:africa_action_result.cancelled_confidence }
			clamp_variable = { var = africa_member_confidence min = constant:africa_measure.minimum max = constant:africa_measure.maximum }
		}
		africa_handle_first_proof_action_resolution = yes
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_id = africa_active_action_id }
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_outcome = africa_active_action_outcome }
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_objective = africa_active_action_objective }
		set_variable = { africa_last_action_generation = africa_active_action_generation }
		set_country_flag = africa_has_action_history
		save_event_target_as = africa_action_result_target
		event_target:africa_host = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr12.220 } }
		africa_cleanup_action = yes
		event_target:africa_host = { africa_focus_ai_retry_pending_route_action = yes }
		event_target:africa_host = { africa_focus_ai_retry_overlay_corridor_action = yes }
	}
}
